对一个泰拉瑞亚复刻项目的液体/半砖子系统做最终正确性审查（找 bug，不是风格）。这些文件刚经历一轮大改，需要新鲜眼睛找逻辑错误、边界条件、变量误用。

审查文件（game/src/ 下）：
1. world/liquid/LiquidSim.ts —— 原版 Liquid.cs 移植。重点：数组越界（idx 计算、±1/±w/±2/±3 邻居访问、liquid Uint8Array 下溢回绕——liquid 是 Uint8Array，任何减法后仍为负再赋值都会回绕成大数！）；sideFlow 各分支对 level 的读写顺序；delWater 的 swap-remove 与循环遍历的交互（updateLiquid 清扫循环 l 从 numLiquid-1 递减、delWater 内 swap）；LiquidBuffer 回灌时的 checking 位管理；settleWaterAt 蛇形循环的死循环风险（dir/step 的变化是否保证终止）。
2. render/VanillaLiquidRenderer.ts —— 重点：at(lx,ly)=lx*ph+ly 布局下所有邻居索引的方向正确性（ui/di/li/ri）；P3 拖尾内层 `at(lx, ly+s)` 的越界；isHalfA 预循环与 P2 半砖分支的交互；P7 内角分支。
3. render/WaterfallRenderer.ts —— 重点：draw 循环的 dx/dy/lastH/turns 状态机（是否存在 dx=dy=0 原地重画死循环、turns 翻转后是否会震荡）；扫描窗口 ±100 与 MAX_FALLS=1000 的交互。
4. physics/TileCollision.ts —— 重点：新加的落地/上顶门槛条件（`b.y + b.h <= top || (b.y + b.h) - dy > top + 0.01` 等）在 dy=0、站立、嵌入学等场景的正确性；`continue` 跳过所有格时 hitY=false 的后果（穿墙？）。
5. world/gen/vanilla/HalfBrickPass.ts —— 重点：setTileSilent 与 st.half 直接写的混用是否会脏残留；runSmoothWorldPass 两遍循环的条件重叠/重复 pound（PoundTile 是 toggle！对同一格 pound 两次会还原——检查是否存在同格多次 pound 的路径）。
6. render/Renderer.ts 的 drawLiquids/drawWaterfalls/_liquidNow 与 render() 中的调用顺序（2a 背景水 → 2b 墙 → 2c tile → 2d 瀑布 → 实体 → 4.5 前景水）——检查 fullMap/return 路径是否跳过了某些层导致状态不一致。

只报确定或高度可疑的 bug：每条给出 文件:行号、问题描述、触发场景。不确定的标注"存疑"。≤40 行。不要报风格/命名/性能小节。
```

31	  findWaterfalls(st: TileStore, tx0: number, ty0: number, tx1: number, ty1: number, frame: number) {

44	      if (t === 0) return false;

88	      let turns = 0;            // num23：方向反转计数

93	        const t = st.type[i];
94	        if (t === 0) return false;

96	        return !!d && d.solid && !d.platform && !st.half[i] && st.slope[i] === 0;
97	      };

99	        if (cx < 1 || cy < 1 || cx >= st.w - 1 || cy >= st.h - 1) break;

107	          turns = 0; dy = 1; dx = 0;

111	          dx = 1; dy = 0; lastH = 1;
112	        } else if ((solidT(cx + 1, cy) || st.liquid[ri] > 0) && (!solidT(cx - 1, cy) && st.liquid[li] === 0)) {

115	          dx = -1; dy = 0; lastH = -1;

117	          dy = 0; dx = lastH;   // 两侧皆空：保持上次水平方向（L492-496）

1	// 原版 1.4.0.5 液体渲染器：Terraria1405/GameContent/Liquid/LiquidRenderer.cs 逐行移植。
2	// 多 pass 网格算法（无任何矩形"浸润"hack）：
3	//  P1 缓存原始液位/实心/墙/类型（±2 padding）
4	//  P2 可见液位：干燥格被左右（或上下）同类型液体夹住 → 两侧液位之和×0.5（原版"透出"效果的本源）
5	//  P3 瀑布拖尾：向下传播 WATERFALL_LENGTH 格，透明度递减（水 10 / 岩浆 3 / 蜂蜜 2）
6	//  P4 四壁插值（Left/Right/Top/BottomWall 0-1）+ 边存在性 + 变体图集 FrameOffset
7	//  P5 壁值平滑（与上下/左右邻取加权均值）
8	//  P6/P7 角落修正（瀑布侧/内角填充）
9	//  绘制：water_N 表（48×1360：3 列变体 × 80px 动画带）按四壁裁源矩形 + 偏移贴图
10	import type { SpriteAtlas } from '../assets/SpriteAtlas';
11	import type { TileStore } from '../world/TileStore';
12	import { TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';
13	
14	const WATERFALL_LENGTH = [10, 3, 2];        // 水岩蜜
15	const DEFAULT_OPACITY = [0.6, 0.95, 0.95];  // 水 / 岩浆 / 蜂蜜（原版常量）
16	
17	// 我们的 liquidType（1 水 / 2 岩浆 / 3 蜂蜜）→ 原版 LiquidType（0/1/2）
18	function toVanillaType(t: number): number {
19	  return t === 2 ? 1 : t === 3 ? 2 : 0;
20	}
21	function waterSheet(vt: number): string {
22	  return vt === 1 ? 'vanilla/Misc_water_1.png' : vt === 2 ? 'vanilla/Misc_water_11.png' : 'vanilla/Misc_water_0.png';
23	}
24	
25	export function drawVanillaLiquids(
26	  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, atlas: SpriteAtlas | null,
27	  st: TileStore, groundLevel: number,
28	  tx0: number, ty0: number, tx1: number, ty1: number,
29	  nowMs: number, isBackground = false,
30	): void {
31	  if (!atlas) return;
32	  const PAD = 2;
33	  const px0 = tx0 - PAD, py0 = ty0 - PAD;
34	  const pw = tx1 - tx0 + 1 + PAD * 2, ph = ty1 - ty0 + 1 + PAD * 2;
35	  const n = pw * ph;
36	  // 平行类型数组（每帧分配，视图 ~5000 格，量级可控）
37	  const level = new Float32Array(n), visLevel = new Float32Array(n), opacity = new Float32Array(n).fill(1);
38	  const isSolidA = new Uint8Array(n), hasLiquidA = new Uint8Array(n), hasWallA = new Uint8Array(n);
39	  const hasVisA = new Uint8Array(n), typeA = new Uint8Array(n), visTypeA = new Uint8Array(n);
40	  const lW = new Float32Array(n), rW = new Float32Array(n), bW = new Float32Array(n), tW = new Float32Array(n);
41	  const vlW = new Float32Array(n), vrW = new Float32Array(n), vbW = new Float32Array(n), vtW = new Float32Array(n);
42	  const hasLE = new Uint8Array(n), hasRE = new Uint8Array(n), hasTE = new Uint8Array(n), hasBE = new Uint8Array(n);
43	  const isHalfA = new Uint8Array(n);
44	  const fx = new Int16Array(n), fy = new Int16Array(n);
45	
46	  // ---- P1：原始缓存 ----
47	  for (let lx = 0; lx < pw; lx++) {
48	    const x = px0 + lx;
49	    for (let ly = 0; ly < ph; ly++) {
50	      const y = py0 + ly;
51	      const i = lx * ph + ly;
52	      if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) { isSolidA[i] = 1; continue; }
53	      const si = st.idx(x, y);
54	      const lq = st.liquid[si];
55	      level[i] = lq / 255;
56	      hasLiquidA[i] = lq > 0 ? 1 : 0;
57	      hasWallA[i] = st.wall[si] > 0 ? 1 : 0;
58	      typeA[i] = toVanillaType(st.liquidType[si]);
59	      const d = TILE_DEFS[st.type[si]];
60	      isSolidA[i] = d && d.solid ? 1 : 0;
61	    }
62	  }

75	      }

77	  }
78	
79	  // ---- P2：可见液位（内区 = 真实视图区） ----
80	  for (let lx = PAD; lx < pw - PAD; lx++) {
81	    for (let ly = PAD; ly < ph - PAD; ly++) {
82	      const i = at(lx, ly);
83	      let v: number;
84	      if (isHalfA[i] && hasLiquidA[at(lx, ly - 1)]) {

86	      } else if (!hasLiquidA[i]) {

90	        if (hasLiquidA[ui] && hasLiquidA[di] && typeA[ui] === typeA[di] && !isSolidA[ui] && !isSolidA[di]) {
91	          val = level[ui] + level[di];
92	          typeA[i] = typeA[ui];
93	        }
94	        if (hasLiquidA[li] && hasLiquidA[ri] && typeA[li] === typeA[ri] && !isSolidA[li] && !isSolidA[ri]) {
95	          val = Math.max(val, level[li] + level[ri]);
96	          typeA[i] = typeA[li];
97	        }
98	        v = val * 0.5;
99	      } else {
100	        v = level[i];
101	      }
102	      visLevel[i] = v;
103	      hasVisA[i] = v !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
104	    }
105	  }
106	
107	  // ---- P3：瀑布拖尾（向下传播） + 实心格处理 ----
108	  for (let lx = 0; lx < pw; lx++) {
109	    for (let ly = 0; ly < ph - 10; ly++) {
110	      const i = at(lx, ly);
111	      if (hasVisA[i] && (!isSolidA[i] || isHalfA[i])) {
112	        opacity[i] = 1;
113	        visTypeA[i] = typeA[i];
114	        const len = WATERFALL_LENGTH[typeA[i]] ?? 3;

130	        hasVisA[i] = 0;

135	    }
136	  }
137	
138	  // ---- P4：四壁插值 + 边存在 + 变体 FrameOffset ----
139	  for (let lx = PAD; lx < pw - PAD; lx++) {
140	    for (let ly = PAD; ly < ph - PAD; ly++) {
141	      const i = at(lx, ly);
142	      if (!hasVisA[i]) { hasLE[i] = hasRE[i] = hasTE[i] = hasBE[i] = 0; continue; }
143	      const ui = at(lx, ly - 1), di = at(lx, ly + 1), li = at(lx - 1, ly), ri = at(lx + 1, ly);
144	      let nT = 0, nB = 1, nL = 0, nR = 1;
145	      const my = visLevel[i];
146	      if (!hasVisA[ui]) nT += visLevel[di] * (1 - my);
147	      if (!hasVisA[di] && !isSolidA[di] && !isHalfA[di]) nB -= visLevel[ui] * (1 - my);
148	      if (!hasVisA[li] && !isSolidA[li] && !isHalfA[li]) nL += visLevel[ri] * (1 - my);
149	      if (!hasVisA[ri] && !isSolidA[ri] && !isHalfA[ri]) nR -= visLevel[li] * (1 - my);
150	      tW[i] = nT; bW[i] = nB; lW[i] = nL; rW[i] = nR;
151	      hasTE[i] = (!hasVisA[ui] && !isSolidA[ui]) || nT !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
152	      hasBE[i] = (!hasVisA[di] && !isSolidA[di]) || nB !== 1 ? 1 : 0;
153	      hasLE[i] = (!hasVisA[li] && !isSolidA[li]) || nL !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
154	      hasRE[i] = (!hasVisA[ri] && !isSolidA[ri]) || nR !== 1 ? 1 : 0;

158	        ox = 16; oy += 32;

160	      } else if (!hasTE[i]) {

163	      }

169	  // ---- P5：壁值平滑 ----
170	  for (let lx = PAD; lx < pw - PAD; lx++) {

172	      const i = at(lx, ly);
173	      if (!hasVisA[i]) continue;

259	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.min(1, opacity[i] * (isBackground ? 1 : (DEFAULT_OPACITY[vt] ?? 0.6)));

263	  }
264	  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;

170	        }
171	      }
172	      // 卡死检测：活动量长期不变 → 清空列表靠唤醒自愈（Liquid.cs:808-824）
173	      if (this.numLiquid > 0 && this.numLiquid > this.stuckAmount - 50 && this.numLiquid < this.stuckAmount + 50) {
174	        this.stuckCount++;
175	        if (this.stuckCount >= 10000) {
176	          this.stuck = true;
177	          for (let l = this.numLiquid - 1; l >= 0; l--) this.delWater(l);
178	          this.stuck = false;
179	          this.stuckCount = 0;
180	        }
181	      } else {
182	        this.stuckCount = 0;
183	        this.stuckAmount = this.numLiquid;
184	      }
185	    }
186	  }
187	
188	  // ================= 单格流程 Update（Liquid.cs:298-674） =================
189	
190	  private update(e: LiquidEntry) {
191	    const st = this.world.store;
192	    const w = st.w, h = st.h;
193	    const x = e.x, y = e.y;
194	    const i5 = this.idx(x, y);
195	    // 1) 本格被实心方块占据 → 下轮必删（Liquid.cs:306-310）
196	    if (this.blocksLiquid(x, y)) { e.kill = 999; return; }
197	    const startAmt = st.liquid[i5];
198	    // 2) 地狱蒸发：水每 tick -2（Liquid.cs:314-320；UnderworldLayer ≈ h-200）
199	    if (y > h - 200 && st.liquidType[i5] === 1 && st.liquid[i5] > 0) {
200	      st.liquid[i5] = Math.max(0, st.liquid[i5] - 2);
201	    }
202	    if (st.liquid[i5] === 0) { e.kill = 999; return; }
203	    const myType = st.liquidType[i5];
204	    // 3) 岩浆/蜂蜜：先交互检查，再降速（Liquid.cs:327-373）
205	    if (myType === 2) {
206	      this.lavaCheck(x, y);
207	      if (!this.quickFall) {
208	        if (e.delay < 5) { e.delay++; return; }
209	        e.delay = 0;
210	      }
211	    } else {
212	      // 水格：唤醒岩浆邻居，让对方自己的 Update 处理交互（Liquid.cs:342-349）
213	      for (const [nx, ny] of [[x - 1, y], [x + 1, y], [x, y - 1], [x, y + 1]] as const) {
214	        if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= w || ny >= h) continue;
215	        const ni = this.idx(nx, ny);
216	        if (st.liquid[ni] > 0 && st.liquidType[ni] === 2) this.addWater(nx, ny);
217	      }
218	      if (myType === 3) {
219	        this.honeyCheck(x, y);
220	        if (!this.quickFall) {
221	          if (e.delay < 10) { e.delay++; return; }
222	          e.delay = 0;
223	        }
224	      } else {
225	        // 唤醒蜂蜜邻居（Liquid.cs:365-372）
226	        for (const [nx, ny] of [[x - 1, y], [x + 1, y], [x, y - 1], [x, y + 1]] as const) {
227	          if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= w || ny >= h) continue;
228	          const ni = this.idx(nx, ny);
229	          if (st.liquid[ni] > 0 && st.liquidType[ni] === 3) this.addWater(nx, ny);
230	        }
231	      }
232	    }
233	    // 4) 向下全量下灌（Liquid.cs:375-397）
234	    {
235	      const bi = this.idx(x, y + 1);
236	      const belowAmt = st.liquid[bi];
237	      const belowType = st.liquidType[bi];
238	      const belowBlocks = y + 1 >= h ? true : this.blocksLiquid(x, y + 1);
239	      if (!belowBlocks && (belowAmt <= 0 || belowType === myType) && belowAmt < 255) {
240	        let t = 255 - belowAmt;
241	        if (t > st.liquid[i5]) t = st.liquid[i5];
242	        // 原版边界特性：缺口 1 且本格满格时不扣源（Liquid.cs:381-384）
243	        const flag = t === 1 && st.liquid[i5] === 255;
244	        if (!flag) st.liquid[i5] -= t;
245	        st.liquid[bi] += t;
246	        st.liquidType[bi] = myType;
247	        this.addWater(x, y + 1);
248	        this.skip[bi] = 1;
249	        this.skip[i5] = 1;
250	        if (this.quickSettle && st.liquid[i5] > 250) st.liquid[i5] = 255;
251	        else if (!flag) { this.addWater(x - 1, y); this.addWater(x + 1, y); }
252	      }
253	    }
254	    // 5) 侧向均流（Liquid.cs:398-651）
255	    if (st.liquid[i5] > 0) this.sideFlow(x, y, i5);
256	    // 6) kill 维护（Liquid.cs:652-671）
257	    if (st.liquid[i5] !== startAmt) {
258	      if (st.liquid[i5] === 254 && startAmt === 255) {
259	        if (this.quickSettle) st.liquid[i5] = 255;
260	        e.kill++;
261	      } else {
262	        this.addWater(x, y - 1);
263	        e.kill = 0;
264	      }
265	    } else {
266	      e.kill++;
267	    }
268	  }
269	
270	  /** 侧向均流：参与格全体写平均值（Liquid.cs:398-651，逐分支照抄） */
271	  private sideFlow(x: number, y: number, i5: number) {
272	    const st = this.world.store;
273	    const myType = st.liquidType[i5];
274	    const il = i5 - 1, ir = i5 + 1, iu = i5 - st.w, im2 = i5 - 2, ip2 = i5 + 2, im3 = i5 - 3, ip3 = i5 + 3;
275	    const lq = st.liquid[il], rq = st.liquid[ir];
276	    // flag1 左可流 / flag2 右可流 / flag3 左 2 格延伸 / flag4 右 2 格延伸
277	    let f1 = true, f2 = true, f3 = true, f4 = true;
278	    if (this.blocksLiquid(x - 1, y)) f1 = false;
279	    else if (lq > 0 && st.liquidType[il] !== myType) f1 = false;
280	    else if (this.blocksLiquid(x - 2, y)) f3 = false;
281	    else if (st.liquid[im2] === 0) f3 = false;
282	    else if (st.liquidType[im2] !== myType) f3 = false;
283	    if (this.blocksLiquid(x + 1, y)) f2 = false;
284	    else if (rq > 0 && st.liquidType[ir] !== myType) f2 = false;
285	    else if (this.blocksLiquid(x + 2, y)) f4 = false;
286	    else if (st.liquid[ip2] === 0) f4 = false;
287	    else if (st.liquidType[ip2] !== myType) f4 = false;
288	    let num1 = 0;
289	    if (st.liquid[i5] < 3) num1 = -1;         // 薄层蒸发偏置（Liquid.cs:424-426）
290	    if (st.liquid[i5] > 250) { f3 = false; f4 = false; }
291	    if (f1 && f2) {
292	      if (f3 && f4) {
293	        // ±3 延伸判定（Liquid.cs:436-449）
294	        let f5 = true, f6 = true;
295	        if (this.blocksLiquid(x - 3, y)) f5 = false;
296	        else if (st.liquid[im3] === 0) f5 = false;
297	        else if (st.liquidType[im3] !== myType) f5 = false;
298	        if (this.blocksLiquid(x + 3, y)) f6 = false;
299	        else if (st.liquid[ip3] === 0) f6 = false;
300	        else if (st.liquidType[ip3] !== myType) f6 = false;
301	        if (f5 && f6) {
302	          // 7 格均分（Liquid.cs:452-515）
303	          const m = Math.round((lq + rq + st.liquid[im2] + st.liquid[ip2] + st.liquid[im3] + st.liquid[ip3] + st.liquid[i5] + num1) / 7);
304	          let same = 0;
305	          if (lq !== m) { st.liquidType[il] = myType; st.liquid[il] = m; this.addWater(x - 1, y); } else { st.liquidType[il] = myType; same++; }
306	          if (rq !== m) { st.liquidType[ir] = myType; st.liquid[ir] = m; this.addWater(x + 1, y); } else { st.liquidType[ir] = myType; same++; }
307	          if (st.liquid[im2] !== m) { st.liquidType[im2] = myType; st.liquid[im2] = m; this.addWater(x - 2, y); } else { st.liquidType[im2] = myType; same++; }
308	          if (st.liquid[ip2] !== m) { st.liquidType[ip2] = myType; st.liquid[ip2] = m; this.addWater(x + 2, y); } else { st.liquidType[ip2] = myType; same++; }
309	          if (st.liquid[im3] !== m) { st.liquidType[im3] = myType; st.liquid[im3] = m; this.addWater(x - 3, y); } else { st.liquidType[im3] = myType; same++; }
310	          if (st.liquid[ip3] !== m) { st.liquidType[ip3] = myType; st.liquid[ip3] = m; this.addWater(x + 3, y); } else { st.liquidType[ip3] = myType; same++; }
311	          // 补偿唤醒：中心可能保持原值，邻居需按"邻居≠均值或中心≠均值"重唤醒（Liquid.cs:502-513）
312	          if (st.liquid[il] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) this.addWater(x - 1, y);
313	          if (st.liquid[ir] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) this.addWater(x + 1, y);
314	          if (st.liquid[im2] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) this.addWater(x - 2, y);
315	          if (st.liquid[ip2] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) this.addWater(x + 2, y);
316	          if (st.liquid[im3] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) this.addWater(x - 3, y);
317	          if (st.liquid[ip3] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) this.addWater(x + 3, y);
318	          // 中心例外：六邻全等于均值且上方有液 → 保持原值（Liquid.cs:514）
319	          if (same !== 6 || st.liquid[iu] <= 0) st.liquid[i5] = m;
320	        } else {
321	          // 5 格均分（Liquid.cs:519-562）
322	          const m = Math.round((lq + rq + st.liquid[im2] + st.liquid[ip2] + st.liquid[i5] + num1) / 5);
323	          let same = 0;
324	          if (lq !== m) { st.liquidType[il] = myType; st.liquid[il] = m; this.addWater(x - 1, y); } else { st.liquidType[il] = myType; same++; }
325	          if (rq !== m) { st.liquidType[ir] = myType; st.liquid[ir] = m; this.addWater(x + 1, y); } else { st.liquidType[ir] = myType; same++; }
326	          if (st.liquid[im2] !== m) { st.liquidType[im2] = myType; st.liquid[im2] = m; this.addWater(x - 2, y); } else { st.liquidType[im2] = myType; same++; }
327	          if (st.liquid[ip2] !== m) { st.liquidType[ip2] = myType; st.liquid[ip2] = m; this.addWater(x + 2, y); } else { st.liquidType[ip2] = myType; same++; }
328	          // 补偿唤醒（Liquid.cs:553-560）
329	          if (st.liquid[il] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) this.addWater(x - 1, y);
330	          if (st.liquid[ir] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) this.addWater(x + 1, y);
331	          if (st.liquid[im2] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) this.addWater(x - 2, y);
332	          if (st.liquid[ip2] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) this.addWater(x + 2, y);
333	          if (same !== 4 || st.liquid[iu] <= 0) st.liquid[i5] = m;
334	        }
335	      } else if (f3) {
336	        // 4 格：左延伸（Liquid.cs:567-587）。类型写在条件判断之前（原版 L568 无条件写）
337	        const m = Math.round((lq + rq + st.liquid[im2] + st.liquid[i5] + num1) / 4);
338	        st.liquidType[il] = myType;
339	        if (lq !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) { st.liquid[il] = m; this.addWater(x - 1, y); }
340	        st.liquidType[ir] = myType;
341	        if (rq !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) { st.liquid[ir] = m; this.addWater(x + 1, y); }
342	        st.liquidType[im2] = myType;
343	        if (st.liquid[im2] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) { st.liquid[im2] = m; this.addWater(x - 2, y); }
344	        st.liquid[i5] = m;
345	      } else if (f4) {
346	        // 4 格：右延伸（Liquid.cs:590-609）。类型写在条件判断之前（原版 L591 无条件写）
347	        const m = Math.round((lq + rq + st.liquid[ip2] + st.liquid[i5] + num1) / 4);
348	        st.liquidType[il] = myType;
349	        if (lq !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) { st.liquid[il] = m; this.addWater(x - 1, y); }
350	        st.liquidType[ir] = myType;
351	        if (rq !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) { st.liquid[ir] = m; this.addWater(x + 1, y); }
352	        st.liquidType[ip2] = myType;
353	        if (st.liquid[ip2] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) { st.liquid[ip2] = m; this.addWater(x + 2, y); }
354	        st.liquid[i5] = m;
355	      } else {
356	        // 3 格均分（Liquid.cs:613-628）：随机 254→255 补满
357	        let m = Math.round((lq + rq + st.liquid[i5] + num1) / 3);
358	        if (m === 254 && Math.random() * 30 < 1) m = 255;
359	        if (lq !== m) { st.liquidType[il] = myType; st.liquid[il] = m; this.addWater(x - 1, y); } else st.liquidType[il] = myType;
360	        if (rq !== m) { st.liquidType[ir] = myType; st.liquid[ir] = m; this.addWater(x + 1, y); } else st.liquidType[ir] = myType;
361	        st.liquid[i5] = m;
362	      }
363	    } else if (f1) {
364	      // 2 格：向左（Liquid.cs:633-639）
365	      const m = Math.round((lq + st.liquid[i5] + num1) / 2);
366	      if (lq !== m) st.liquid[il] = m;
367	      st.liquidType[il] = myType;
368	      if (st.liquid[i5] !== m || st.liquid[il] !== m) this.addWater(x - 1, y);
369	      st.liquid[i5] = m;
370	    } else if (f2) {
371	      // 2 格：向右（Liquid.cs:643-649）
372	      const m = Math.round((rq + st.liquid[i5] + num1) / 2);
373	      if (rq !== m) st.liquid[ir] = m;
374	      st.liquidType[ir] = myType;
375	      if (st.liquid[i5] !== m || st.liquid[ir] !== m) this.addWater(x + 1, y);
376	      st.liquid[i5] = m;
377	    }
378	  }
379	
380	  // ================= 液体交互（Liquid.cs:888-1115） =================
381	
382	  /** 岩浆格与水/蜂蜜接触 → 黑曜石 56 / 脆蜂蜜块 230（阈值 24） */
383	  private lavaCheck(x: number, y: number) {
384	    const st = this.world.store;
385	    if (this.solidTileFull(x, y)) return; // WorldGen.SolidTile：半砖/坡面放行（Liquid.cs:898）
386	    const i5 = this.idx(x, y);
387	    const il = i5 - 1, ir = i5 + 1, iu = i5 - st.w, id = i5 + st.w;
388	    const lq = st.liquid[il], rq = st.liquid[ir], uq = st.liquid[iu], dq = st.liquid[id];
389	    const lt = st.liquidType[il], rt = st.liquidType[ir], ut = st.liquidType[iu], dt = st.liquidType[id];
390	    if ((lq > 0 && lt !== 2) || (rq > 0 && rt !== 2) || (uq > 0 && ut !== 2)) {
391	      // 情形 A：左右/上方有非岩浆液体 → 生成在岩浆格（Liquid.cs:905-959）
392	      let sum = 0;
393	      let type = OBSIDIAN;
394	      if (lt !== 2) { sum += lq; st.liquid[il] = 0; }
395	      if (rt !== 2) { sum += rq; st.liquid[ir] = 0; }
396	      if (ut !== 2) { sum += uq; st.liquid[iu] = 0; }
397	      if (lt === 3 || rt === 3 || ut === 3) type = CRISPY_HONEY;
398	      if (sum < 24) return;
399	      const t = st.type[i5];
400	      if (t !== 0) {
401	        const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
402	        if (d && d.decor) st.setTile(x, y, 0);  // 近似 tileObsidianKill
403	        else return;                             // 平台等保留方块 → 不生成
404	      }
405	      st.liquid[i5] = 0;
406	      st.liquidType[i5] = 0;
407	      st.setTile(x, y, type);
408	    } else {
409	      if (dq <= 0 || dt === 2) return;
410	      // 情形 B：仅下方有水/蜂蜜 → 生成在下方格（Liquid.cs:961-1014）
411	      const belowT = st.type[id];
412	      if (belowT !== 0) {
413	        const d = TILE_DEFS[belowT];
414	        if (d && d.decor) st.setTile(x, y + 1, 0); // tileCut / obsidianKill 近似
415	        else return;
416	      }
417	      if (st.liquid[i5] < 24) { st.liquid[i5] = 0; st.liquidType[i5] = 0; return; }
418	      const type = dt === 3 ? CRISPY_HONEY : OBSIDIAN;
419	      st.liquid[i5] = 0; st.liquidType[i5] = 0;
420	      st.liquid[id] = 0; st.liquidType[id] = 0;
421	      st.setTile(x, y + 1, type);
422	    }
423	  }
424	
425	  /** 蜂蜜格与水接触 → 蜂蜜块 229（阈值 32） */
426	  private honeyCheck(x: number, y: number) {
427	    const st = this.world.store;
428	    if (this.solidTileFull(x, y)) return; // WorldGen.SolidTile：半砖/坡面放行（Liquid.cs:1020）
429	    const i5 = this.idx(x, y);
430	    const il = i5 - 1, ir = i5 + 1, iu = i5 - st.w, id = i5 + st.w;
431	    const lq = st.liquid[il], rq = st.liquid[ir], uq = st.liquid[iu], dq = st.liquid[id];
432	    const lt = st.liquidType[il], rt = st.liquidType[ir], ut = st.liquidType[iu], dt = st.liquidType[id];
433	    if ((lq > 0 && lt === 1) || (rq > 0 && rt === 1) || (uq > 0 && ut === 1)) {
434	      let sum = 0;
435	      if (lt === 1) { sum += lq; st.liquid[il] = 0; }
436	      if (rt === 1) { sum += rq; st.liquid[ir] = 0; }
437	      if (ut === 1) { sum += uq; st.liquid[iu] = 0; }
438	      if (sum < 32) return;
439	      const t = st.type[i5];
440	      if (t !== 0) {
441	        const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
442	        if (d && d.decor) st.setTile(x, y, 0);
443	        else return;
444	      }
445	      st.liquid[i5] = 0;
446	      st.liquidType[i5] = 0;
447	      st.setTile(x, y, HONEY_BLOCK);
448	    } else {
449	      if (dq <= 0 || dt !== 1) return;
450	      const belowT = st.type[id];
451	      if (belowT !== 0) {
452	        const d = TILE_DEFS[belowT];
453	        if (d && d.decor) st.setTile(x, y + 1, 0);
454	        else return;
455	      }
456	      if (st.liquid[i5] < 32) { st.liquid[i5] = 0; st.liquidType[i5] = 0; return; }
457	      st.liquid[i5] = 0; st.liquidType[i5] = 0;
458	      st.liquid[id] = 0; st.liquidType[id] = 0;
459	      st.setTile(x, y + 1, HONEY_BLOCK);
460	    }
461	  }
462	
463	  // ================= DelWater（Liquid.cs:1117-1213） =================
464	
465	  private delWater(l: number) {
466	    const st = this.world.store;
467	    const e = this.liquids[l];
468	    const x = e.x, y = e.y;
469	    const i5 = this.idx(x, y);
470	    const il = i5 - 1, ir = i5 + 1, id = i5 + st.w;
471	    const amt = st.liquid[i5];
472	    if (amt < 2) {
473	      // 残量清零 + 邻居唤醒（Liquid.cs:1125-1137）
474	      st.liquid[i5] = 0;
475	      if (st.liquid[il] < 2) st.liquid[il] = 0; else this.addWater(x - 1, y);
476	      if (st.liquid[ir] < 2) st.liquid[ir] = 0; else this.addWater(x + 1, y);
477	    } else if (amt < 20) {
478	      // 水膜抹除：邻格更低或下方不满（Liquid.cs:1138-1142）
479	      const lq = st.liquid[il], rq = st.liquid[ir], dq = st.liquid[id];
480	      if ((lq < amt && !this.blocksLiquid(x - 1, y)) ||
481	          (rq < amt && !this.blocksLiquid(x + 1, y)) ||
482	          (dq < 255 && !this.blocksLiquid(x, y + 1))) {
483	        st.liquid[i5] = 0;
484	      }
485	    } else if (st.liquid[id] < 255 && !this.blocksLiquid(x, y + 1) && !this.stuck && !this.blocksLiquid(x, y)) {
486	      e.kill = 0; // 下方未满：留在列表继续流（Liquid.cs:1143-1147）
487	      return;
488	    }
489	    if (st.liquid[i5] < 250 && st.liquid[i5 - st.w] > 0) this.addWater(x, y - 1);
490	    if (st.liquid[i5] === 0) {
491	      st.liquidType[i5] = 0;
492	    } else {
493	      const rq = st.liquid[ir], lq = st.liquid[il];
494	      if (rq > 0 && rq < 250 && !this.blocksLiquid(x + 1, y) && st.liquid[i5] !== rq) this.addWater(x + 1, y);
495	      if (lq > 0 && lq < 250 && !this.blocksLiquid(x - 1, y) && st.liquid[i5] !== lq) this.addWater(x - 1, y);
496	      if (st.liquidType[i5] === 2) {
497	        this.lavaCheck(x, y);
498	        // 3×3 草转泥土近似：岩浆旁 decor 植物清除（Liquid.cs:1160-1187 原版为草方块转换）
499	        for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
500	          for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
501	            const nx = x + dx, ny = y + dy;
502	            if (nx < 1 || ny < 1 || nx >= st.w - 1 || ny >= st.h - 1) continue;
503	            const ni = this.idx(nx, ny);
504	            const d = TILE_DEFS[st.type[ni]];
505	            if (d && d.decor && d.attach === 'ground') st.setTile(nx, ny, 0);
506	          }
507	        }
508	      } else if (st.liquidType[i5] === 3) {
509	        this.honeyCheck(x, y);
510	      }
511	    }
512	    // swap-remove（Liquid.cs:1193-1197）：只搬 x/y/kill，delay 保留本槽旧值（原版语义）
513	    this.numLiquid--;
514	    this.checking[i5] = 0;
515	    const tail = this.liquids[this.numLiquid];
516	    e.x = tail.x; e.y = tail.y; e.kill = tail.kill;

518	  }
519	
520	  // ================= 读档沉降：QuickWater（Liquid.cs:85-103 / 105-212） =================
521	
522	  /** 自底向上逐湿格直接搬运沉降（原版 QuickWater(verbose, -1, -1)：y 从 h-3 到 3） */
523	  quickWater(minY = 3, maxY = -1) {
524	    const st = this.world.store;
525	    const yMax = maxY < 0 ? st.h - 3 : maxY;
526	    for (let y = yMax; y >= minY; y--) {
527	      for (let x = 4; x < st.w - 4; x++) {
528	        if (st.liquid[this.idx(x, y)] !== 0) this.settleWaterAt(x, y);
529	      }
530	    }
531	  }
532	
533	  /** 单格液体直接搬到最终落点（Liquid.cs:105-212 逐行对照） */
534	  private settleWaterAt(originX: number, originY: number) {
535	    const st = this.world.store;
536	    const oi = this.idx(originX, originY);
537	    if (st.liquid[oi] === 0) return;
538	    let X = originX, Y = originY;
539	    const srcType = st.liquidType[oi];
540	    let liquid = st.liquid[oi];
541	    st.liquid[oi] = 0;
542	    let flag1 = true;
543	    for (;;) {
544	      // 1) 垂直下落：下方空且可通行就一直落（Liquid.cs:121-130）
545	      let flag2 = false;
546	      while (Y < st.h - 5 && st.liquid[this.idx(X, Y + 1)] === 0 && !this.blocksLiquid(X, Y + 1)) {
547	        Y++;
548	        flag2 = true;
549	        flag1 = false;
550	      }
551	      // （Liquid.cs:129-130 的 waterLine 岩浆转换仅世界生成期生效，读档跳过）
552	      // 2) 蛇形横向铺开（Liquid.cs:131-195）
553	      let dir = -1;          // num2：当前行走方向
554	      let step = 0;          // num3：当前行步数
555	      let lastDir = -1;      // num4：最后空位方向
556	      let lastStep = 0;      // num5：最后空位步数
557	      let hitL = false;      // flag4：左边界
558	      let hitR = false;      // flag3：右边界
559	      let dropped = false;   // flag5：本轮发生下落
560	      for (;;) {
561	        const ci = this.idx(X + step * dir, Y);
562	        if (st.liquid[ci] === 0) { lastDir = dir; lastStep = step; }
563	        if (dir === -1 && X + step * dir < 5) hitL = true;
564	        else if (dir === 1 && X + step * dir > st.w - 5) hitR = true;
565	        const bi = this.idx(X + step * dir, Y + 1);
566	        // 侧下方同类型未满 → 直接灌入（Liquid.cs:149-159）
567	        if (st.liquid[bi] !== 0 && st.liquid[bi] !== 255 && st.liquidType[bi] === srcType) {
568	          let t = 255 - st.liquid[bi];
569	          if (t > liquid) t = liquid;
570	          st.liquid[bi] += t;
571	          liquid -= t;
572	          if (liquid === 0) break;
573	        }
574	        if (Y >= st.h - 5 || st.liquid[bi] !== 0 || this.blocksLiquid(X + step * dir, Y + 1)) {
575	          // 下方堵住 → 沿行继续 / 折返（Liquid.cs:162-191）
576	          const ni = this.idx(X + (step + 1) * dir, Y);
577	          const nAmt = st.liquid[ni];
578	          const nSolid = this.blocksLiquid(X + (step + 1) * dir, Y);
579	          if ((nAmt !== 0 && (!flag1 || dir !== 1)) || nSolid) {
580	            if (dir === 1) hitR = true; else hitL = true;
581	          }
582	          if (hitL && hitR) break;
583	          if (hitR) { dir = -1; step++; }
584	          else if (hitL) { if (dir === 1) step++; dir = 1; }
585	          else { if (dir === 1) step++; dir = -dir; }
586	        } else {
587	          dropped = true;
588	          break;
589	        }
590	      }
591	      X += lastStep * lastDir;
592	      if (liquid !== 0 && dropped) Y++;
593	      else break;
594	    }
595	    // 3) 写回落点（Liquid.cs:204-205）
596	    const fi = this.idx(X, Y);
597	    st.liquid[fi] = liquid;
598	    st.liquidType[fi] = srcType;
599	    if (st.liquid[fi] > 0) {
600	      // 落地即交互（Liquid.cs:206-210 / AttemptToMoveLava/Honey 248-280）
601	      this.attemptToMoveLiquid(X, Y, srcType, 2);
602	      this.attemptToMoveLiquid(X, Y, srcType, 3);
603	    }
604	  }
605	
606	  /** 落点与异种液体相邻时触发交互检查（Liquid.cs:248-280 AttemptToMoveLava 同构）。
607	   *  原版语义：邻居的 target 性 != 落点格的 target 性 才算"异种相遇"——
608	   *  水落水旁不算，水落岩浆旁才算（否则整片海洋互相当岩浆转黑曜石） */
609	  private attemptToMoveLiquid(X: number, Y: number, srcType: number, targetType: number) {
610	    const st = this.world.store;
611	    const srcIs = srcType === targetType;
612	    const check = targetType === 2 ? (x: number, y: number) => this.lavaCheck(x, y)
613	      : (x: number, y: number) => this.honeyCheck(x, y);
614	    const diff = (x: number, y: number) => {
615	      const i = this.idx(x, y);
616	      return st.liquid[i] > 0 && (st.liquidType[i] === targetType) !== srcIs;
617	    };
618	    if (diff(X - 1, Y)) { if (srcIs) check(X, Y); else check(X - 1, Y); }
619	    else if (diff(X + 1, Y)) { if (srcIs) check(X, Y); else check(X + 1, Y); }
620	    else if (diff(X, Y - 1)) { if (srcIs) check(X, Y); else check(X, Y - 1); }
621	    else if (diff(X, Y + 1)) { if (srcIs) check(X, Y); else check(X, Y + 1); }
622	  }
623	
624	  // ================= WaterCheck（WorldGen.cs:48955-49009） =================
625	
626	  /** 全图自底向上重建活动列表：能流的/液面不齐的/岩浆旁有异种的都入列 */
627	  waterCheck() {
628	    const st = this.world.store;
629	    this.liquids.length = 0;
630	    this.numLiquid = 0;
631	    this.checking.fill(0);
632	    for (let x = 1; x < st.w - 1; x++) {
633	      for (let y = st.h - 2; y > 0; y--) {
634	        const i = this.idx(x, y);
635	        if (st.liquid[i] === 0) continue;
636	        const t = st.type[i];
637	        if (t !== 0) {
638	          const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
639	          if (d && d.solid && !d.platform) { st.liquid[i] = 0; continue; } // 实心格内液体清零
640	        }
641	        const id = i + st.w;
642	        const dq = st.liquid[id];
643	        if (!this.blocksLiquid(x, y + 1) && dq < 255) {
644	          if (dq > 250) st.liquid[id] = 255;
645	          else this.addWater(x, y);
646	        }
647	        const il = i - 1, ir = i + 1;
648	        if (!this.blocksLiquid(x - 1, y) && st.liquid[il] !== st.liquid[i]) this.addWater(x, y);
649	        else if (!this.blocksLiquid(x + 1, y) && st.liquid[ir] !== st.liquid[i]) this.addWater(x, y);
650	        if (st.liquidType[i] === 2) {
651	          const iu = i - st.w;
652	          if ((st.liquid[il] > 0 && st.liquidType[il] !== 2) ||
653	              (st.liquid[ir] > 0 && st.liquidType[ir] !== 2) ||
654	              (st.liquid[iu] > 0 && st.liquidType[iu] !== 2) ||
655	              (dq > 0 && st.liquidType[id] !== 2)) this.addWater(x, y);
656	        }
657	      }
658	    }
659	  }
660	}

1	// 分轴扫描 tile AABB 碰撞
2	import { TILE } from '../core/constants';
3	import type { World } from '../world/World';
4	
5	export interface Body {
6	  x: number; y: number;       // 左上角（px）
7	  w: number; h: number;
8	  vx: number; vy: number;
9	  onGround: boolean;
10	  hitWall: boolean;
11	  hitHead: boolean;
12	  /** 平台单向碰撞：仅当开启且下落时与平台碰撞 */
13	  dropThrough?: boolean;
14	}
15	
16	/** 格子的半砖碰撞盒（原版 Collision.cs:1320-1324 三处一致）：下半 8px。
17	 *  返回 null = 非实心；[top, bottom] = 碰撞盒的像素 y 区间 */
18	function solidSpan(world: World, tx: number, ty: number): [number, number] | null {
19	  const st = world.store;
20	  if (!st.isSolid(tx, ty)) return null;
21	  const top = ty * TILE + (st.half[st.idx(tx, ty)] ? 8 : 0);
22	  return [top, (ty + 1) * TILE];
23	}
24	
25	/** 单个轴的移动 + 碰撞解析。返回是否发生碰撞。 */
26	function moveAxis(b: Body, world: World, dx: number, dy: number): { hitX: boolean; hitY: boolean } {
27	  let hitX = false, hitY = false;
28	  // X 轴
29	  if (dx !== 0) {
30	    b.x += dx;
31	    const dir = Math.sign(dx);
32	    const edgeX = dir > 0 ? b.x + b.w : b.x;
33	    const tx = Math.floor(edgeX / TILE);
34	    const y0 = Math.floor(b.y / TILE), y1 = Math.floor((b.y + b.h - 0.01) / TILE);
35	    for (let ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++) {
36	      const span = solidSpan(world, tx, ty);
37	      // 半砖只占下半：身体底部没超过半砖顶面（ty*16+8）则不拦
38	      if (span && b.y + b.h > span[0]) {
39	        if (dir > 0) b.x = tx * TILE - b.w;
40	        else b.x = (tx + 1) * TILE;
41	        b.vx = 0;
42	        hitX = true;
43	        break;
44	      }
45	    }
46	  }
47	  // Y 轴
48	  if (dy !== 0) {
49	    b.y += dy;
50	    const dir = Math.sign(dy);
51	    const edgeY = dir > 0 ? b.y + b.h : b.y;
52	    const ty = Math.floor(edgeY / TILE);
53	    const x0 = Math.floor(b.x / TILE), x1 = Math.floor((b.x + b.w - 0.01) / TILE);
54	    for (let tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {
55	      const solid = world.store.isSolid(tx, ty);
56	      const span = solidSpan(world, tx, ty);
57	      const plat = !b.dropThrough && dir > 0 && world.store.isPlatform(tx, ty)
58	        && (b.y + b.h) - dy <= ty * TILE + 1; // 上一位置在平台之上
59	      if (solid || plat) {
60	        if (dir > 0) {
61	          // 落地门槛（Collision.cs:1610/1631）：新底部越过盒顶 且 上一位置在盒顶之上，
62	          // 否则（嵌入/侧入）不吸附——半砖盒顶是 ty*16+8，只进入行上半不算落地
63	          const top = span ? span[0] : ty * TILE;
64	          if (b.y + b.h <= top || (b.y + b.h) - dy > top + 0.01) continue;
65	          b.y = top - b.h;
66	          b.onGround = true;
67	        } else {
68	          // 上顶：上一位置在盒底之下才命中
69	          const bottom = (ty + 1) * TILE;
70	          if (b.y >= bottom || b.y - dy < bottom - 0.01) continue;
71	          b.y = bottom;
72	          b.hitHead = true;
73	        }
74	        b.vy = 0;
75	        hitY = true;
76	        break;
77	      }
78	    }
79	  }
80	  return { hitX, hitY };
81	}
82	
83	/** 移动并碰撞。高速时分子步防穿透。 */
84	export function moveAndCollide(b: Body, world: World, dx: number, dy: number) {
85	  b.onGround = false;
86	  b.hitWall = false;
87	  b.hitHead = false;
88	  const maxStep = 8;
89	  const steps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dy)) / maxStep));
90	  for (let i = 0; i < steps; i++) {
91	    const sx = dx / steps, sy = dy / steps;
92	    const { hitX, hitY } = moveAxis(b, world, sx, sy);
93	    if (hitX) { b.hitWall = true; dx = 0; }
94	    if (hitY) dy = 0;
95	    if (dx === 0 && dy === 0) break;
96	  }
97	}
98	

```

17	export function solidTile(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
18	  if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= st.w || y >= st.h) return false;
19	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
20	  const t = st.type[i];
21	  if (t === 0) return false;
22	  const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
23	  return !!d && d.solid && !d.platform && !st.half[i] && st.slope[i] === 0;
24	}

26	/** 可砸判定（WorldGen.cs:49122 CanPoundTile 简化）：实心非平台非框架物 */
27	function canPoundTile(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
28	  const t = st.type[st.idx(x, y)];
29	  if (t === 0) return false;
30	  const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
31	  return !!d && d.solid && !d.platform && !d.framed;
32	}

35	function poundTile(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
36	  if (!canPoundTile(st, x, y)) return false;
37	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
38	  st.half[i] = st.half[i] ? 0 : 1;
39	  if (st.half[i]) st.slope[i] = 0;
40	  return true;
41	}

148	      const num3 = 8 + Math.floor(_rng.next() * 12), num4 = 8 + Math.floor(_rng.next() * 12);
149	      for (let k = j - num3; k <= j + num4; k++) {
150	        if (k >= 0 && k < h && st.half[st.idx(i, k)]) flag = false;
151	      }
152	      if (flag) poundTile(st, i, j);
153	    }
154	  }
155	  // —— 第二遍（L7725-7741）：唇缘向相邻实心格扩展半砖 ——
156	  for (let i = 20; i < w - 20; i++) {
157	    for (let j = 20; j < h - 20; j++) {
158	      const ti = st.idx(i, j);
159	      if (!solidTile(st, i, j) || !solidTile(st, i, j + 1)) continue;
160	      if (!solidTile(st, i + 1, j) && st.half[st.idx(i - 1, j)] && st.liquid[st.idx(i - 2, j)] > 0) {
161	        poundTile(st, i, j);
162	      }
163	      if (!solidTile(st, i - 1, j) && st.half[st.idx(i + 1, j)] && st.liquid[st.idx(i + 2, j)] > 0) {
164	        poundTile(st, i, j);
165	      }
166	    }
167	  }
168	}

Key differences found in LavaCheck port vs vanilla:

**Vanilla case A (port lines 390-407):** After `if (num < 24) return;` — vanilla zeroes neighbor liquids BEFORE the check too (tile1.liquid = 0 inside accumulation). So the port matches vanilla here — vanilla also destroys liquid if sum < 24. Wait, yes: vanilla sets `tile1.liquid = 0` during accumulation, then `if (num < 24) return;`. So neighbor liquid destroyed even when no obsidian forms. Port matches. Hmm interesting, so that's vanilla behavior, not a bug.

BUT: important difference — in case A, the type conversion: vanilla checks `tile1.honey() || tile2.honey() || tile3.honey()` → Type 230. Port line 397: `if (lt === 3 || rt === 3 || ut === 3) type = CRISPY_HONEY;` — same.

**Vanilla case A placement check:** `if (tile5.active()) return;` — only generates obsidian if the lava cell is EMPTY. The port: line 399-404: if tile type is decor → setTile 0 (kill); else return. And also `if (t !== 0)` → if active and NOT obsidianKill → vanilla would have killed it first (KillTile) if tileObsidianKill. So port approximates: decor cleared, others block. OK — documented deviation.

**Vanilla case B:** obsidian placed at (x, y+1) with `tile4` requirements: `if (!(!tile4.active() | flag)) return;` — i.e., tile4 must be inactive (or container flag). Port: line 411-415: if below type non-zero and decor → kill; else return. Same approximation. But note: in vanilla case B, `tile5.liquid` (the lava) is only consumed if >= 24; if < 24, `tile5.liquid = 0; tile5.liquidType(0)` — lava destroyed. Port line 417: `if (st.liquid[i5] < 24) { st.liquid[i5] = 0; st.liquidType[i5] = 0; return; }` ✓.

But — CRITICAL: vanilla case B checks `tile4.liquid > 0 && !tile4.lava()` first. Port: line 409 `if (dq <= 0 || dt === 2) return;` ✓.

Note vanilla case B also requires the check `tile4` not active happens BEFORE the `tile5.liquid < 24` check. Port order: line 411-416 (below tile kill/block check) then line 417 (lava < 24). Same order. ✓

    public static void AddWater(int x, int y)
    {
      Tile checkTile = Main.tile[x, y];
      if (Main.tile[x, y] == null || checkTile.checkingLiquid() || (x >= Main.maxTilesX - 5 || y >= Main.maxTilesY - 5) || (x < 5 || y < 5 || checkTile.liquid == (byte) 0) || checkTile.nactive() && Main.tileSolid[(int) checkTile.type] && (checkTile.type != (ushort) 546 && !Main.tileSolidTop[(int) checkTile.type]))
        return;
      if (Liquid.numLiquid >= Liquid.curMaxLiquid - 1)
      {
        LiquidBuffer.AddBuffer(x, y);
      }
      else
      {
        checkTile.checkingLiquid(true);
        checkTile.skipLiquid(false);
        Main.liquid[Liquid.numLiquid].kill = 0;
        Main.liquid[Liquid.numLiquid].x = x;
        Main.liquid[Liquid.numLiquid].y = y;
        Main.liquid[Liquid.numLiquid].delay = 0;
        ++Liquid.numLiquid;
        if (Main.netMode == 2)
          Liquid.NetSendLiquid(x, y);
        if (!checkTile.active() || WorldGen.gen)
          return;
        bool flag = false;
        if (checkTile.lava())
        {
          if (TileObjectData.CheckLavaDeath(checkTile))
            flag = true;
        }
        else if (TileObjectData.CheckWaterDeath(checkTile))
          flag = true;
        if (!flag)
          return;
        WorldGen.KillTile(x, y, false, false, false);
        if (Main.netMode != 2)
          return;
        NetMessage.SendData(17, -1, -1, (NetworkText) null, 0, (float) x, (float) y, 0.0f, 0, 0, 0);
      }
    }

    public static void QuickWater(int verbose = 0, int minY = -1, int maxY = -1)

      if (minY == -1)
        minY = 3;
      if (maxY == -1)
        maxY = Main.maxTilesY - 3;
      for (int index = maxY; index >= minY; --index)
      {
        Liquid.UpdateProgressDisplay(verbose, minY, maxY, index);
        for (int originX = 4; originX < Main.maxTilesX - 4; ++originX)
        {
          if (Main.tile[originX, index].liquid != (byte) 0)
            Liquid.SettleWaterAt(originX, index);
        }
      }
      Liquid.tilesIgnoreWater(false);
    }

    private static void SettleWaterAt(int originX, int originY)
    {
      Tile tile1 = Main.tile[originX, originY];
      Liquid.tilesIgnoreWater(true);
      if (tile1.liquid == (byte) 0)
        return;
      int X = originX;
      int Y = originY;
      bool tileAtXYHasLava = tile1.lava();
      bool tileAtXYHasHoney = tile1.honey();
      int liquid = (int) tile1.liquid;
      byte num1 = tile1.liquidType();
      tile1.liquid = (byte) 0;
      bool flag1 = true;
      while (true)
      {
        Tile tile2 = Main.tile[X, Y + 1];
        bool flag2 = false;
        for (; Y < Main.maxTilesY - 5 && tile2.liquid == (byte) 0 && (!tile2.nactive() || !Main.tileSolid[(int) tile2.type] || Main.tileSolidTop[(int) tile2.type]); tile2 = Main.tile[X, Y + 1])
        {
          ++Y;
          flag2 = true;
          flag1 = false;
        }
        if (flag2 && WorldGen.gen && (!tileAtXYHasHoney && Y > WorldGen.waterLine))
          num1 = (byte) 1;
        int num2 = -1;
        int num3 = 0;
        int num4 = -1;
        int num5 = 0;
        bool flag3 = false;
        bool flag4 = false;
        bool flag5 = false;
        while (true)
        {
          if (Main.tile[X + num3 * num2, Y].liquid == (byte) 0)
          {
            num4 = num2;
            num5 = num3;
          }
          if (num2 == -1 && X + num3 * num2 < 5)
            flag4 = true;
          else if (num2 == 1 && X + num3 * num2 > Main.maxTilesX - 5)
            flag3 = true;
          Tile tile3 = Main.tile[X + num3 * num2, Y + 1];
          if (tile3.liquid != (byte) 0 && tile3.liquid != byte.MaxValue && (int) tile3.liquidType() == (int) num1)
          {
            int num6 = (int) byte.MaxValue - (int) tile3.liquid;
            if (num6 > liquid)
              num6 = liquid;
            tile3.liquid += (byte) num6;
            liquid -= num6;
            if (liquid == 0)
              goto label_34;
          }
          if (Y >= Main.maxTilesY - 5 || tile3.liquid != (byte) 0 || tile3.nactive() && Main.tileSolid[(int) tile3.type] && !Main.tileSolidTop[(int) tile3.type])
          {
            Tile tile4 = Main.tile[X + (num3 + 1) * num2, Y];
            if (tile4.liquid != (byte) 0 && (!flag1 || num2 != 1) || tile4.nactive() && Main.tileSolid[(int) tile4.type] && !Main.tileSolidTop[(int) tile4.type])

              if (num2 == 1)
                flag3 = true;
              else

      if (Main.tile[X, Y].liquid > (byte) 0)
      {
        Liquid.AttemptToMoveLava(X, Y, tileAtXYHasLava);
        Liquid.AttemptToMoveHoney(X, Y, tileAtXYHasHoney);
      }
      Liquid.tilesIgnoreWater(false);
    }

    private static void AttemptToMoveHoney(int X, int Y, bool tileAtXYHasHoney)
    {
      if (Main.tile[X - 1, Y].liquid > (byte) 0 && Main.tile[X - 1, Y].honey() != tileAtXYHasHoney)
      {
        if (tileAtXYHasHoney)
          Liquid.HoneyCheck(X, Y);
        else
          Liquid.HoneyCheck(X - 1, Y);
      }
      else if (Main.tile[X + 1, Y].liquid > (byte) 0 && Main.tile[X + 1, Y].honey() != tileAtXYHasHoney)
      {
        if (tileAtXYHasHoney)
          Liquid.HoneyCheck(X, Y);
        else
          Liquid.HoneyCheck(X + 1, Y);
      }
      else if (Main.tile[X, Y - 1].liquid > (byte) 0 && Main.tile[X, Y - 1].honey() != tileAtXYHasHoney)
      {
        if (tileAtXYHasHoney)
          Liquid.HoneyCheck(X, Y);
        else
          Liquid.HoneyCheck(X, Y - 1);
      }
      else
      {
        if (Main.tile[X, Y + 1].liquid <= (byte) 0 || Main.tile[X, Y + 1].honey() == tileAtXYHasHoney)
          return;
        if (tileAtXYHasHoney)
          Liquid.HoneyCheck(X, Y);
        else
          Liquid.HoneyCheck(X, Y + 1);
      }
    }

    private static void AttemptToMoveLava(int X, int Y, bool tileAtXYHasLava)

      else if (Main.tile[X + 1, Y].liquid > (byte) 0 && Main.tile[X + 1, Y].lava() != tileAtXYHasLava)

        if (tileAtXYHasLava)

      }
      else if (Main.tile[X, Y - 1].liquid > (byte) 0 && Main.tile[X, Y - 1].lava() != tileAtXYHasLava)
      {

          Liquid.LavaCheck(X, Y);
        else
          Liquid.LavaCheck(X, Y - 1);
      }
      else

          return;
        if (tileAtXYHasLava)
          Liquid.LavaCheck(X, Y);
        else
          Liquid.LavaCheck(X, Y + 1);
      }
    }

        float num = (float) (maxY - y) / (float) (maxY - minY + 1) / (float) -verbose;
        Main.statusText = Lang.gen[18].Value + " " + (object) (int) ((double) num * 100.0 + 1.0) + "%";
      }
    }

34	  idx(x: number, y: number): number { return y * this.w + x; }
35	
36	  inBounds(x: number, y: number): boolean {
37	    return x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < this.w && y < this.h;

40	  get(x: number, y: number): number { return this.inBounds(x, y) ? this.type[this.idx(x, y)] : 0; }

42	  isActive(x: number, y: number): boolean {
43	    return this.inBounds(x, y) && this.type[this.idx(x, y)] !== 0;
44	  }
45	
46	  isSolid(x: number, y: number): boolean {
47	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return false; // 世界外视为空（边缘自由）
48	    const d = TILE_DEFS[this.type[this.idx(x, y)]];
49	    return !!d && d.solid;
50	  }
51	
52	  isPlatform(x: number, y: number): boolean {

58	  onTileChanged(fn: (x: number, y: number) => void) {

62	  /** 液体变化独立通道：chunk 缓存不订阅（水不入烘焙画布，无需重建），

69	  /** 唯一的写入入口（生成/导入期可绕过用 setTileSilent） */
70	  setTile(x: number, y: number, type: number, frameX = 0, frameY = 0) {
71	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
72	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
73	    this.type[i] = type;
74	    this.flags[i] = type !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
75	    this.frameX[i] = frameX;
76	    this.frameY[i] = frameY;
77	    if (type === 0) { this.half[i] = 0; this.slope[i] = 0; } // 挖除清半砖/坡面
78	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));

83	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
84	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
85	    this.half[i] = v ? 1 : 0;
86	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
87	  }
88	
89	  setSlope(x: number, y: number, s: number) {

94	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
95	  }

102	    this.frameX[i] = frameX;

105	  }

108	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;

112	      this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));

120

122	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
123	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
124	    const a = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, amount | 0));
125	    if (this.liquid[i] === a && this.liquidType[i] === type) return;
126	    this.liquid[i] = a;

128	    this.liquidListeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));

Note: LiquidSim writes `st.liquid[i]` and `st.liquidType[i]` DIRECTLY (not via setLiquid) — so no onLiquidChanged events fire during simulation → the LiquidSim constructor subscribes via onLiquidChanged for bucket placement etc., fine, but lighting subscribed to onLiquidChanged never gets updates during flow. ```

Port line 105: `if ((!solidT(cx, cy + 1) || st.type[bi] === 0) && !st.half[ci]) { turns = 0; dy = 1; dx = 0; }` — vanilla: `(!SolidTile(below) && !below.bottomSlope() && !half) || (!below.active() && !half)`. Port: `(!solidT(below) || type[bi] === 0) && !half`. solidT requires active (type!==0) so `!solidT(below)` ≈ `!SolidTile(below)` (ignoring bottomSlope which is slope 3/4 — the port says slope branches omitted). And `st.type[bi] === 0` ≈ `!below.active()`. The vanilla OR: `(!SolidTile && !bottomSlope && !half) || (!active && !half)` → effectively `!half && (!SolidTile || !active)`. Port: `(!solidT || !active) && !half` ✓ equivalent (slope omitted, documented).

2. `num21 = tile.liquid / 16` — then used for cropping: let me check how num21 is used in drawing (the port uses `sh = 16 - floor(liq/16)`). Let me read further to see the actual drawImage and also `if (liquid > 0 && !half) break` equivalent. Let me look at the end of the loop.

448	    private unsafe void InternalDraw(
449	      SpriteBatch spriteBatch,
450	      Vector2 drawOffset,
451	      int waterStyle,
452	      float globalAlpha,
453	      bool isBackgroundDraw)
454	    {
455	      Rectangle drawArea = this._drawArea;
456	      Main.tileBatch.Begin();
457	      fixed (LiquidRenderer.LiquidDrawCache* liquidDrawCachePtr1 = &this._drawCache[0])
458	      {
459	        LiquidRenderer.LiquidDrawCache* liquidDrawCachePtr2 = liquidDrawCachePtr1;
460	        for (int x = drawArea.X; x < drawArea.X + drawArea.Width; ++x)
461	        {
462	          for (int y = drawArea.Y; y < drawArea.Y + drawArea.Height; ++y)
463	          {
464	            if (liquidDrawCachePtr2->IsVisible)
465	            {
466	              Rectangle sourceRectangle = liquidDrawCachePtr2->SourceRectangle;
467	              if (liquidDrawCachePtr2->IsSurfaceLiquid)
468	                sourceRectangle.Y = 1280;
469	              else
470	                sourceRectangle.Y += this._animationFrame * 80;
471	              Vector2 liquidOffset = liquidDrawCachePtr2->LiquidOffset;
472	              float val2 = liquidDrawCachePtr2->Opacity * (isBackgroundDraw ? 1f : LiquidRenderer.DEFAULT_OPACITY[(int) liquidDrawCachePtr2->Type]);
473	              int index = (int) liquidDrawCachePtr2->Type;
474	              switch (index)
475	              {
476	                case 0:
477	                  index = waterStyle;
478	                  val2 *= globalAlpha;
479	                  break;
480	                case 2:
481	                  index = 11;
482	                  break;
483	              }
484	              float num = Math.Min(1f, val2);
485	              VertexColors vertices;
486	              Lighting.GetCornerColors(x, y, out vertices, 1f);
487	              vertices.BottomLeftColor *= num;
488	              vertices.BottomRightColor *= num;
489	              vertices.TopLeftColor *= num;
490	              vertices.TopRightColor *= num;
491	              Main.DrawTileInWater(drawOffset, x, y);
492	              Main.tileBatch.Draw(this._liquidTextures[index].Value, new Vector2((float) (x << 4), (float) (y << 4)) + drawOffset + liquidOffset, new Rectangle?(sourceRectangle), vertices, Vector2.Zero, 1f, SpriteEffects.None);
493	            }
494	            ++liquidDrawCachePtr2;
495	          }
496	        }
497	      }
498	      Main.tileBatch.End();
499	    }

501	    public bool HasFullWater(int x, int y)
502	    {
503	      x -= this._drawArea.X;
504	      y -= this._drawArea.Y;
505	      int index = x * this._drawArea.Height + y;
506	      if (index < 0 || index >= this._drawCache.Length)
507	        return true;
508	      return this._drawCache[index].IsVisible && !this._drawCache[index].IsSurfaceLiquid;
509	    }
510	
511	    public float GetVisibleLiquid(int x, int y)
512	    {
513	      x -= this._drawArea.X;
514	      y -= this._drawArea.Y;
515	      if (x < 0 || x >= this._drawArea.Width || (y < 0 || y >= this._drawArea.Height))
516	        return 0.0f;
517	      int index = (x + 2) * (this._drawArea.Height + 4) + y + 2;
518	      return !this._cache[index].HasVisibleLiquid ? 0.0f : this._cache[index].VisibleLiquidLevel;
519	    }

522	    {
523	      if (Main.gamePaused || !Main.hasFocus)
524	        return;
525	      float num = Main.windSpeedCurrent * 25f;
526	      this._frameState += ((double) num >= 0.0 ? num + 6f : num - 6f) * (float) gameTime.ElapsedGameTime.TotalSeconds;
527	      if ((double) this._frameState < 0.0)
528	        this._frameState += 16f;
529	      this._frameState %= 16f;
530	      this._animationFrame = (int) this._frameState;
531	    }

Vanilla else (HasLeftEdge): vlW = DOWN's vlW; vtW = RIGHT's vtW. Port line 195: `vlW[i] = vlW[di]; vtW[i] = vtW[ri];` ✓.

**P6 second branch (port line 196-199)** vs vanilla L311-323: `else if (liquidCache2.FrameOffset.X == 16 && liquidCache2.FrameOffset.Y == 32)` — liquidCache2 = DOWN. Port: `else if (fx[di] === 16 && fy[di] === 32)` ✓.

`if (VisibleLeftWall > 0.5) { VisibleLeftWall = 0; FrameOffset = (0,0); } else if (VisibleRightWall < 0.5) { VisibleRightWall = 1; FrameOffset = (32, 0); }` ✓ port matches.

Second branch: `liquidCache4.HasTopEdge && liquidCache1.HasRightEdge` — liquidCache4 = RIGHT, liquidCache1 = UP → `right.HasTopEdge && up.HasRightEdge`. PORT line 215: `hasTE[ri] && hasRE[ui]` ✓.
FrameOffset.X = 32 - Math.Min(16, (int)(up.VisibleRightWall*16) - 4) ✓ port line 216 (vrW[ui]) ✓. Y = 48 + Math.Max(4, (int)(16 - right.VisibleTopWall*16)) - 4 → port uses vtW[ri] ✓.

**P4 wall naming (port 143-154) vs vanilla L198-223:**
Vanilla: liquidCache1 = [-1] = up, liquidCache2 = [+1] = down, liquidCache3 = [-H] = left, liquidCache4 = [+H] = right.
```
num2 = 0 (LeftWall), num3 = 1 (RightWall), num4 = 0 (TopWall), num5 = 1 (BottomWall)
if (!up.HasVisibleLiquid) num4 += down.VisibleLiquidLevel * (1 - my);          // TopWall
if (!down.HasVisibleLiquid && !down.IsSolid && !down.IsHalfBrick) num5 -= up.VisibleLiquidLevel * (1-my);   // BottomWall
if (!left.HasVisibleLiquid && !left.IsSolid && !left.IsHalfBrick) num2 += right.VisibleLiquidLevel * (1-my); // LeftWall
if (!right.HasVisibleLiquid && !right.IsSolid && !right.IsHalfBrick) num3 -= left.VisibleLiquidLevel * (1-my); // RightWall
LeftWall = num2; RightWall = num3; BottomWall = num5; TopWall = num4;
```

if (!hasVisA[ri] && !isSolidA[ri] && !isHalfA[ri]) nR -= visLevel[li] * (1 - my);
```
✓ faithful. Edges (151-154) ✓ match (up/di for top/bottom; li/ri for left/right) ✓.

Wait — port line 151: `hasTE[i] = (!hasVisA[ui] && !isSolidA[ui]) || nT !== 0` — vanilla: `HasTopEdge = !liquidCache1.HasVisibleLiquid && !liquidCache1.IsSolid || num4 != 0` (up) ✓. hasBE uses di ✓, hasLE uses li ✓, hasRE uses ri ✓.

So VanillaLiquidRenderer is faithful! Impressive. Now the details that DIFFER:

**P2 loop ranges**: vanilla index1 from 2 to rect.Width-2, index2 from 2 to rect.Height-2 — where rectangle includes padding (width = drawArea.Width + 4). So index1 ∈ [2, padWidth-2) — i.e., processes cells with 2 cells of margin. Port: lx ∈ [PAD, pw-PAD) where pw = tw + 4 → [2, tw+2) ✓ same.

But vanilla processes cells in y order within a column so [-1] is the already-processed previous row ✓. Port pre-loop iterates ly from 1 → same ✓.

BUT: vanilla's loop starts `liquidCachePtr2 = liquidCachePtr1` at rectangle start — for the FIRST row of the column (y = rect.Y), `liquidCachePtr2[-1]` points to... the cache has an extra element at index 0 (cache[1] is the first). `liquidCachePtr2[-1]` for the very first cell = cache[0] which is uninitialized/garbage (allocated array = zeros in C# → HasLiquid=false). Another: **`opacity` array initialized to 1 and `visTypeA` only set in P3 for cells with visible liquid** — vanilla: VisibleType set in P3 only too ✓. OK — I'm fairly convinced VanillaLiquidRenderer is faithful. One thing to double check: the port's P3 writes `opacity[bi] = k` and `visTypeA[bi] = typeA[i]` — vanilla writes VisibleType = ptr->Type ✓.

Vanilla semantics:

**Landing (down):** vanilla condition `vector2_4.Y + Height <= vector2_5.Y` — i.e., the PREVIOUS position's bottom was AT OR ABOVE the tile top (allowing equality). vector2_4 = old position. So vanilla lands when `oldBottom <= tileTop`. Port line 64: `if (b.y + b.h <= top || (b.y + b.h) - dy > top + 0.01) continue;` — continue (skip) when newBottom <= top (no overlap — correct, no collision possible... wait, is it? If newBottom <= top, the body doesn't reach the tile → skip correct) OR `newBottom - dy > top + 0.01` i.e. oldBottom > top + 0.01 → skip (was already below the top → side entry/embedded → don't snap up). So the port lands when oldBottom <= top + 0.01. Vanilla lands when oldBottom <= top (exactly). The 0.01 tolerance is fine (vanilla uses exact <=).

**But here's the important difference**: vanilla's condition for the whole collision test at the top: `vector2_3.Y + Height > vector2_5.Y && vector2_3.Y < vector2_5.Y + num14` — the new position must actually overlap the tile's box (for half brick, the box is the lower 8px: vector2_5.Y += 8, num14 = 8). And if there's NO overlap, no collision at all. The port: `if (span && b.y + b.h > span[0])` for X axis — checks newBottom > spanTop. But does it check `b.y < span[1]` (bottom of tile)? For the Y axis, the port uses `ty = floor(edgeY / TILE)` — only the edge row!           moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);

        this.vy -= 0.32;
        this.vy *= 0.92;
        this.vx *= 0.95;
        moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
        return;
      }
    }
    this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + GRAVITY, MAX_FALL_SPEED);
    this.vx = Math.max(-maxSpd, Math.min(maxSpd, this.vx + accel * Math.sign(this.vx || 1) * 0));
    if (jumpV && this.onGround) this.vy = -jumpV;
    moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
    this.vx *= this.onGround ? 0.8 : 0.98;
  }

  // 全屏地图查看器状态（zoom 向 zoomTarget 缓动；缓动期间按锚点补偿 pan）
  fullMap = {
    open: false, zoom: 0.5, zoomTarget: 0.5, panX: 0, panY: 0,
    anchorU: 0, anchorV: 0, anchorMX: 0, anchorMY: 0,
  };

  /** 全屏地图缩放：以鼠标位置为锚点（鼠标下的地图点不动，不乱飞） */
  zoomFullMapAt(newZoom: number, mouseX: number, mouseY: number) {
    const fm = this.fullMap;
    const viewW = this.canvas.width, viewH = this.canvas.height;
    const clamped = Math.max(0.5, Math.min(6, newZoom));
    // 记录锚点：鼠标下的地图源坐标 + 鼠标屏幕位置。
    // 缓动期间每帧按公式 pan = anchorMX - viewW/2 + W*zoom/2 - u*zoom 重解，
    // 保证缓动全程锚点不动（否则缓动中 pan 固定会让地图"自己跑"）
    const cx0 = viewW / 2 - (this._fmWorldW * fm.zoom) / 2 + fm.panX;
    const cy0 = viewH / 2 - (this._fmWorldH * fm.zoom) / 2 + fm.panY;
    fm.anchorU = (mouseX - cx0) / fm.zoom;
    fm.anchorV = (mouseY - cy0) / fm.zoom;
    fm.anchorMX = mouseX;
    fm.anchorMY = mouseY;
    fm.zoomTarget = clamped;
    this.applyMapAnchor();
  }

  /** 按锚点反解 pan（当前 zoom 下鼠标处的地图点固定在鼠标下） */
  private applyMapAnchor() {
    const fm = this.fullMap;
    const viewW = this.canvas.width, viewH = this.canvas.height;
    fm.panX = fm.anchorMX - viewW / 2 + (this._fmWorldW * fm.zoom) / 2 - fm.anchorU * fm.zoom;
    fm.panY = fm.anchorMY - viewH / 2 + (this._fmWorldH * fm.zoom) / 2 - fm.anchorV * fm.zoom;
  }

  /** 每帧缓动 fullMap.zoom → zoomTarget；缓动期间同步按锚点补偿 pan */
  easeFullMap() {
    const fm = this.fullMap;
    const diff = fm.zoomTarget - fm.zoom;
    if (Math.abs(diff) < 0.002) { fm.zoom = fm.zoomTarget; return; }
    fm.zoom += diff * 0.16;
    this.applyMapAnchor();
  }

  }

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-09T04:18:20.834Z · glm-x-preview-260804

```
以下是对照 `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1405/` 反编译源逐行核对的审查结果（行号为当前文件行号）。

## 确定 bug

**1. `game/src/world/liquid/LiquidSim.ts:561-597` — settleWaterAt 蛇形循环 X 越界后 idx 行回绕，液体写进错误行**
蛇形探测 `this.idx(X + step*dir, Y)` 的边界哨兵（`X+step*dir < 5` / `> w-5`）只置 sticky 标志，`step` 在 flip 后仍持续增长。当 X 远离世界中轴（如 X=100、w=4200）：hitL 在 step≈96 触发后，右侧探测的 step 继续涨到 >4095，左侧探测坐标变为约 -3995——`idx()` 落到**上一行的合法数组下标**（不是 undefined！），读到的是真实但错误的格子；若该格 `liquid===0` 会被记为 lastDir/lastStep（562 行），随后 591 行 `X += lastStep*lastDir` 把 X 甩到 [0,w) 之外，597 行 `st.liquid[fi] = liquid` 把液体写进**另一行**，同时源格液体已在 541 行被清零。触发：quickWater 读档沉降时，液体停在靠近世界边缘的宽大平面上（海洋底/大洞穴底，下方整行被堵、两侧开阔）。修法：对 `X+step*dir` 做与哨兵一致的 [5,w-5] 钳制。

**2. `game/src/render/WaterfallRenderer.ts:117-134` — 四面皆堵时 dx=dy=0 原地重画**
`else { turns++; dy = 0; dx = 0; }` 后若 `turns < 2`，`cx += dx; cy += dy` 不动，下一轮同一格再次命中同一分支；`turns >= 2` 后 `dx *= -1` 仍是 -0，于是同一格被重画至多 ~97 次（alpha 0.6 叠到近全不透明，末 10 步再叠淡出）。原版对应路径（WaterfallManager.cs case 0，`num26=0&&num27=0`）画一次后 `index4=1000` 直接终止水柱。触发：水柱落进两侧实心、下方实心的 1 格宽凹槽（很常见）。注意 `dx = lastH` 分支（116 行）不会为 0——唇缘格必先走分支 2/3 把 lastH 置 ±1。

**3. `game/src/physics/TileCollision.ts:54-76` — Y 轴落地取"最左列"而非"最高面"，跨半砖/整砖会卡进地面**
循环 `break` 在第一个可解析的 tx（最左列），而原版用 num13/num8 机制取**最高的盒顶**（Collision.cs:1610-1631：`num13 > vector2_5.Y` 才更新）。身体左列是半砖、右列是整砖下落时，port 吸附到半砖面（ty*16+8），右半身体嵌入整砖 8px；下一帧 64 行门槛 `(b.y+b.h)-dy > top+0.01`（旧底=ty*16+8 > ty*16）对该列永久 skip，X 轴又因 `b.y+b.h > span[0]` 持续挡墙 → 角色半截陷地 + 卡墙。修法：遍历整行取最小 `top` 再吸附。

## 高度可疑 / 偏离原版

**4. `game/src/world/gen/vanilla/HalfBrickPass.ts:62,66` — 模式 A/B 多出原版没有的 `type(i∓1,j)===0` 条件**
原版 L7600/7608 只要求 `!SolidTile(i∓1,j)`（WorldGen.cs:7577-7580），邻居是半砖/坡面时仍会 pound；port 额外要求该格完全无方块 → 半砖邻接地形少生成半砖，瀑布唇缘变少。属移植走样非崩溃。

**5. `game/src/world/liquid/LiquidSim.ts:629-631` — waterCheck 不清空 this.buffer**
原版 WaterCheck 首行 `LiquidBuffer.numLiquidBuffer = 0`（WorldGen.cs:48959），port 重建活动列表时 buffer 残留，下个清扫轮回灌陈旧条目（addWater 有 liquid>0 校验所以只是浪费，非破坏）。同文件 165 行回灌量用剩余空位 `curMaxLiquid - numLiquid`，原版 799 行实为 `numLiquid`（`curMaxLiquid-(curMaxLiquid-numLiquid)`）——行为偏离但更合理，存疑不计。

**6. `game/src/render/WaterfallRenderer.ts:131` — 水平段取样胶片左半帧**
所有段都用 `drawImage(texImg, frameX, 0, 16, sh, ...)`（32px 帧的左半）；原版垂直段用左半（`x2,0,16,16-num21`）但水平/转折段用右半 `16+x2` 并 FlipHorizontally（WaterfallManager.cs:663-742）→ 水平跑段贴图错。存疑（垂直主流路径正确）。

**7. `game/src/render/Renderer.ts:384 + 236` — 背景水 pass 用上一帧的 _liquidNow**
`_liquidNow` 只在 2d 的 drawWaterfalls 里更新，2a 背景液体在它之前执行 → 两 pass 时间戳差一帧，动画帧切换瞬间背景层与前景 0.6 层帧号不一致（371 行注释声称共用同一时刻）。fullMap 的 return（332 行）只跳过 HUD，世界各层均在检查点之前画完，无层丢失。

## 已核对无问题（对照原版确认）

- LiquidSim：`liquid -= t`/`+= t` 均有 t≤存量与 ≤255 约束，无 Uint8 回绕；sideFlow 七分支读写顺序与 Liquid.cs:436-649 逐行一致（含中心最后写、补偿唤醒、`same!==6` 例外）；delWater 的 swap-remove 与清扫循环 l 递减方向配合正确（尾元素必已访问过）；buffer 回灌先清 checking 再 addWater，位不泄漏；lavaCheck/honeyCheck "sum<24 时邻格液体已被清零"是原版同款行为（Liquid.cs:924-941）。
- VanillaLiquidRenderer：at() 列主序下 ui/di/li/ri 全部方向正确，P2-P7 与 InternalDraw 源矩形/clamp/帧偏移与 LiquidRenderer.cs:83-499 逐行吻合，P3 `at(lx,ly+s)` 有 `ly+s>=ph` 先行 break，无列回绕。未发现 bug。
- HalfBrickPass：setTileSilent(0) 清 half/slope、pound 目标均先过 solidTile（half=0），无脏残留；第二遍双条件互斥，无同格双 pound；lip pass 第二遍两条独立 if 的潜在双 toggle 与原版 L7732-7737 结构相同（继承原版，非移植引入）。
